{
  "skill": "spec-plan",
  "args": "integrate the following knowledge When working with Claude on coding projects, context management is crucial. Your project might have dozens or hundreds of files, but Claude only needs the right information to help you effectively. Too much irrelevant context actually decreases Claude's performance, so learning to guide it toward relevant files and documentation is essential.\n\n\n\nThe /init Command\nWhen you first start Claude in a new project, run the /init command. This tells Claude to analyze your entire codebase and understand:\n\nThe project's purpose and architecture\nImportant commands and critical files\nCoding patterns and structure\n\n\nAfter analyzing your code, Claude creates a summary and writes it to a CLAUDE.md file. When Claude asks for permission to create this file, you can either hit Enter to approve each write operation, or press Shift+Tab to let Claude write files freely throughout your session.\n\nThe CLAUDE.md File\nThe CLAUDE.md file serves two main purposes:\n\nGuides Claude through your codebase, pointing out important commands, architecture, and coding style\nAllows you to give Claude specific or custom directions\nThis file gets included in every request you make to Claude, so it's like having a persistent system prompt for your project.\n\nCLAUDE.md File Locations\nClaude recognizes three different CLAUDE.md files in three common locations:\n\n\n\nCLAUDE.md - Generated with /init, committed to source control, shared with other engineers\nCLAUDE.local.md - Not shared with other engineers, contains personal instructions and customizations for Claude\n~/.claude/CLAUDE.md - Used with all projects on your machine, contains instructions that you want Claude to follow on all projects\nAdding Custom Instructions\nYou can customize how Claude behaves by adding instructions to your CLAUDE.md file. For example, if Claude is adding too many comments to code, you can address this by updating the file.\n\nUse the # command to enter \"memory mode\" - this lets you edit your CLAUDE.md files intelligently. Just type something like:\n\n# Use comments sparingly. Only comment complex code.\nClaude will merge this instruction into your CLAUDE.md file automatically.\n\nFile Mentions with '@'\nWhen you need Claude to look at specific files, use the @ symbol followed by the file path. This automatically includes that file's contents in your request to Claude.\n\nFor example, if you want to ask about your authentication system and you know the relevant files, you can type:\n\nHow does the auth system work? @auth\nClaude will show you a list of auth-related files to choose from, then include the selected file in your conversation.\n\nReferencing Files in CLAUDE.md\nYou can also mention files directly in your CLAUDE.md file using the same @ syntax. This is particularly useful for files that are relevant to many aspects of your project.\n\nFor example, if you have a database schema file that defines your data structure, you might add this to your CLAUDE.md:\n\nThe database schema is defined in the @prisma/schema.prisma file. Reference it anytime you need to understand the structure of data stored in the database.\nWhen you mention a file this way, its contents are automatically included in every request, so Claude can answer questions about your data structure immediately without having to search for and read the schema file each time. --worktree=yes"
}

   135→## Coding Standards (Compound-Ready)
   136→
   137→- **Type hints**: Mandatory (mypy --strict). Enables alignment analysis at compile-time
   138→- **Docstrings**: NumPy-style. Document "why" intent, state assumptions for request alignment
   139→- **Async**: All I/O must be `async/await` with timeouts. No blocking calls in executors
   140→- **Error handling**: Specific exceptions + circuit breakers (`cohezion.reliability.get_circuit()`)
   141→- **Validation**: Pydantic at boundaries (input/output). Fail fast with assertions
   142→- **KISS**: Simple logic beats multi-agent swarms. Measure first, optimize later
   143→- **Every `src/` dir**: MUST have `__init__.py`. Enables vault skill discovery
   144→- **Observability**: Log state transitions (input → processing → output). Track coherence. Measure alignment
   145→- **Lazy imports for optional deps**: Never top-level import matplotlib/numpy/heavy ML libs in API route files — they cascade via conftest and break all tests. Use lazy imports inside endpoint functions
   146→- **Dev deps**: Use `uv sync --extra dev` (not bare `uv sync`) to include pytest/ruff/etc.
   147→
   148→### Journey Tracking Checklist (Compound Loop)
   149→When implementing features, add:
   150→1. **Input logging**: `journey_tracker.record_request(alignment_score)` at entry
   151→2. **State changes**: Record before/after for rollback capability
   152→3. **Metrics**: Call `metrics_collector.record_execution()` at completion
   153→4. **Coherence**: Check `degradation_detector.check_coherence()` before proceeding
   154→5. **Reflection**: Populate RetrospectionEngine output for skill refinement

   156→### Alignment Assessment (Before Execution)
   157→```python
   158→from cohezion.compound.request_alignment_analyzer import RequestAlignmentAnalyzer
   159→
   160→analyzer = RequestAlignmentAnalyzer()
   161→alignment = analyzer.analyze(request_state, available_skills, agent_context)
   162→if alignment.coherence < 0.5:  # HIHO threshold
   163→    logger.warning(f"Low coherence: {alignment.issues}")
   164→    # Escalate or fallback
   165→```
   166→
   167→## Token Budgets (Conservative Estimates)
   168→
   169→| Task | Tokens | Pattern |
   170→|------|--------|---------|
   171→| Implement 1 feature | 500-1,500 | Copy template → code → manual test → 5 tests |
   172→| Research + implement | 2,000-3,000 | Quick survey → proof of concept → tests |
   173→| Full test suite run | 100 | `uv run pytest tests/ -q` (no code changes) |
   174→| Single test debug | 200-500 | Add print → run → check output → fix → verify |
   175→| Skill refinement loop | 1,500-2,500 | Analyze failures → update PRIME skill → retest |
   176→| **ANTI-PATTERN**: Research-first | 5,000-10,000 | 1,200 lines research + 600 tests + 0 implementation = wasted |
   177→| **ANTI-PATTERN**: Infrastructure play | 8,000+ | Dependency research, 4,400 placeholder tests, product doesn't exist |
   178→
   179→**Rule**: If feature doesn't exist yet, DON'T build infrastructure. Implement, validate, THEN test.
   180→
   181→## Operational Patterns
   182→
   183→### ⚡ Test Isolation (Critical)
   184→Tests fail only as full suite? **NOT a logic bug — singleton pollution**. Fix in `tests/conftest.py`:
   185→- FLUME VAE: `cohezion.api._vae_trainer = None` (checkpoint mismatch causes NaN)
   186→- RL Policy: `cohezion.api._rl_policy = None` (bad reward state persists)
   187→- Loggers: `logging.getLogger().handlers.clear()` (formatters corrupt across files)
   188→
   189→### ⚡ Mocking External Services
   190→```python
   191→# CORRECT: Mock at source
   192→@patch("cohezion.swarm.compound_client.get_compound_client")
   193→
   194→# WRONG: Mocking after import fails randomly
   195→with patch("cohezion.api.compound_client"):  # Import already happened
   196→```
   197→
   198→### ⚡ Measurement Integrity
   199→- **Report actual numbers**: 2,675/2,700 passing (98.1%) > 1,599 passing (inflated)
   200→- **Flag discrepancies**: If independent test shows different count, request re-verification immediately
   201→- **Verify claims**: "Complete" = files exist AND tests pass, not projected
   202→
   203→### ⚡ Verification Protocol
   204→Before declaring task complete:
   205→1. **Code**: Reads logically? Type hints present?
   206→2. **Tests**: `uv run pytest tests/module/ -v` passes? No warnings?
   207→3. **Metrics**: Honest count? Zero regressions?
   208→4. **Alignment**: Does code match what was requested?
   209→5. **Rollback**: Can prior version recover? (State transitions logged?)
   210→
   211→## Hardware & Constraints (Strix Halo)
   212→
   213→- **CPU**: AMD Ryzen AI MAX+ 395 (16C/32T, AVX-512, AMX) | **GPU**: Radeon 8060S (iGPU, unified memory)
   214→- **RAM**: 128 GiB LPDDR5X | **Storage**: 2TB NVMe + 32GB swap (ZFS)
   215→- **Local Models**: Ollama (deepseek-r1:70b, qwen3-coder:30b, phi3:mini). **Global limit = 4 concurrent**
   216→- **Cost**: Cloud Run = Free Tier only (no instances when idle). Prefer local Ollama over API calls
   217→- **Truth Anchor**: See `HARDWARE_PROFILE_PRIME.md` (never assume RTX/CUDA)
   218→
   219→## Multi-Session Worktree Pattern (MANDATORY)
   220→
   221→**Every Claude session creates an isolated feature branch to prevent conflicts and data loss.**
   222→
   223→```bash
   224→# Session startup
   225→SESSION_ID="55"
   226→PHASE="test-fixes"
   227→git worktree add ~/dev/cohezion-session-${SESSION_ID} -b session-${SESSION_ID}-${PHASE}
   228→cd ~/dev/cohezion-session-${SESSION_ID}
   229→
   230→# Session work: One goal, atomic commits
   231→uv run pytest tests/ -q  # Verify baseline
   232→# ... make changes, test incrementally ...
   233→
   234→# Session cleanup
   235→git commit -m "Session ${SESSION_ID}: ${PHASE}
   236→
   237→## Accomplishments
   238→- [Deliverables + test count/%, regressions: zero]
   239→
   240→## For Session ${SESSION_ID+1}
   241→- [Key assumptions, remaining work, gotchas]"
   242→
   243→git push origin session-${SESSION_ID}-${PHASE}
   244→cd ~/dev/cohezion && git worktree remove ~/dev/cohezion-session-${SESSION_ID}
   245→```
   246→
   247→**Why**: Isolation → no conflicts | Reversibility → safe branching | Audit trail → clear history | Safety → main never edited directly
   248→
   249→**Git Rules** (see `.claude/rules/git-workflow.md`):
   250→- Never force-push to main
   251→- Conventional commits: `feat:`, `fix:`, `test:`, `refactor:`, `chore:`
   252→- AI commits include: `Co-Authored-By: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>`
   253→- No files >1MB (use git-lfs)
   254→- Check `git status` before any commit
   255→
   256→## Design Principles (Compound-Aligned)
   257→
   258→- **HIHO Stability** (50% coherence): Optimal balance between exploitation + exploration
   259→- **Compound Engineering**: Every feature makes future features easier (loop → skill updates → better routing)
   260→- **Observable AI**: Log states before action. Track coherence. Expose confidence. Enable rollback
   261→- **Deterministic Responsibility**: Idempotency keys for all external calls. Replay from checkpoints
   262→- **Expert Domain Lattice**: Route complex requests through specialist agents (Architect, Engineer, Biologist, QHW, QAlgo)
   263→- **SPIN Coherence**: Information unit = Rotation + Precession. Alignment when phases match
   264→
   265→## Critical References (Read First)
   266→
   267→| Document | Purpose | Key Insight |
   268→|----------|---------|------------|
   269→| `tests/conftest.py` | **TEST ISOLATION** | FLUME VAE + RL policy + logger resets prevent flaky tests |
   270→| `.agent/CONSTITUTION.md` | **HARD CONSTRAINTS** | No WMD/infrastructure attacks. Honesty mandatory. Idempotency required |
   271→| `.agent/COHEZION_CHARTER.md` | **DESIGN THEORY** | SPIN, FLUME, HIHO, Expert Domain Lattice |
   272→| `HARDWARE_PROFILE_PRIME.md` | **TRUTH ANCHOR** | Never assume RTX/CUDA. AMD Ryzen AI MAX+ 395 only |
   273→| `REDACTED.md` | **LESSONS EXTRACTED** | Historical patterns, anti-patterns, cost lessons |
   274→| `.claude/rules/git-workflow.md` | **GIT PATTERNS** | Conventional commits, branch naming, PR targets |
   275→| `.claude/rules/testing.md` | **TEST RULES** | Avoid `walk_packages`, mock at source, HIHO invariant |
   276→| `cloud-vault-mcp/` | **MCP TEMPLATE** | 40+ tools, FastMCP proven. Copy when building MCP servers |
   277→
   278→## Agent Journey Tracking (Compound Loop Observability)
   279→
   280→**Every agent action must be trackable through 12D universe. Required for skill refinement and drift detection.**
   281→
   282→### Journey Entry Point
   283→```python
   284→from cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker
   285→
   286→tracker = JourneyTracker()
   287→state_before = tracker.record_state(
   288→    agent_id="researcher-1",
   289→    phase="research",  # {research, planning, execution, reflection}
   290→    position={"x": 0.5, "y": 0.3, ...},  # 12D coordinates
   291→    coherence=0.85,  # Agent's skill coherence
   292→    context=request_state  # Input to this phase
   293→)
   294→```
   295→
   296→### Checkpoints (Non-Blocking)
   297→```python
   298→# Record at state transitions (try/except to prevent crashes)
   299→try:
   300→    tracker.record_transition(
   301→        state_before,
   302→        action_taken,
   303→        result,
   304→        coherence_after=0.83,
   305→        alignment_score=0.92  # How well action matched request
   306→    )
   307→except Exception as e:
   308→    logger.warning(f"Journey tracking failed (non-blocking): {e}")
   309→```
   310→
   311→### Recovery Checkpoint (Rollback Path)
   312→```python
   313→# Before executing irreversible action, save checkpoint
   314→checkpoint = tracker.save_checkpoint(
   315→    agent_id="researcher-1",
   316→    phase="execution",
   317→    state=current_state
   318→)
   319→# ... execute ...
   320→if failure:
   321→    tracker.rollback_to_checkpoint(checkpoint)
   322→```
   323→
   324→### Query Journey (Debugging + Skill Refinement)
   325→```python
   326→# Retrospection engine uses this to refine skills
   327→journey = tracker.get_journey(agent_id="researcher-1")
   328→anomalies = tracker.detect_anomalies(journey)  # Drift, coherence collapse
   329→for anomaly in anomalies:
   330→    logger.info(f"Anomaly: {anomaly.phase} → coherence {anomaly.before} → {anomaly.after}")
   331→```
   332→
   333→## Request Alignment Assessment (Before Execution)
   334→
   335→**Every request must be assessed for alignment with available skills and agent context. Prevents wasted tokens on misaligned tasks.**
   336→
   337→### Alignment Analysis Pipeline
   338→```python
   339→from cohezion.compound.request_alignment_analyzer import RequestAlignmentAnalyzer
   340→from cohezion.compound.skill_selector import SkillSelector
   341→
   342→analyzer = RequestAlignmentAnalyzer()
   343→selector = SkillSelector()
   344→
   345→# 1. Parse request and check available skills
   346→request = parse_request(user_input)  # {goal, constraints, context}
   347→available_skills = selector.find_relevant_skills(request.keywords)
   348→
   349→# 2. Assess alignment
   350→alignment = analyzer.analyze(
   351→    request=request,
   352→    available_skills=available_skills,
   353→    agent_coherence=agent.coherence_history,  # Historical performance
   354→    computational_budget=5000  # Tokens available
   355→)
   356→
   357→# 3. Make routing decision
   358→if alignment.coherence < 0.5:  # HIHO threshold
   359→    logger.warning(f"Low alignment: {alignment.issues}")
   360→    action = "escalate" or "decompose"  # Break into smaller requests
   361→elif alignment.estimated_tokens > budget:
   362→    action = "batch_or_defer"
   363→else:
   364→    action = "proceed"  # Execute with confidence
   365→    selected_skill = alignment.best_matching_skill
   366→```
   367→
   368→### Alignment Score Components
   369→- **Coherence** (0.0-1.0): How well request matches agent's expertise
   370→- **Completeness** (0.0-1.0): Are all required params present?
   371→- **Constraint Satisfaction** (0.0-1.0): Can execution honor time/token/resource constraints?
   372→- **Drift Risk** (0.0-1.0): How much could this destabilize coherence?
   373→- **Estimated Tokens**: Projection for cost budgeting
   374→
   375→### Anti-Patterns
   376→- ❌ Accept ANY request without alignment check (wastes tokens)
   377→- ❌ Proceed with coherence <0.5 (HIHO collapse)
   378→- ❌ Ignore computational_budget (tokens explode)
   379→- ❌ Skip drift detection (coherence decays)
   380→
   381→## Metrics & Observability (Production Monitoring)
   382→
   383→**Global metrics track efficiency, cost, and quality across all agents and executions.**
   384→
   385→### Recording Metrics
   386→```python
   387→from cohezion.compound.global_metrics_aggregator import GlobalMetricsAggregator
   388→
   389→agg = GlobalMetricsAggregator()
   390→
   391→# Record after each execution
   392→agg.record_execution(
   393→    instance_metrics={
   394→        "executions": 1,
   395→        "tokens_used": 1250,
   396→        "coherence": 0.87,
   397→        "cache_hit_rate": 0.95,
   398→        "cost_usd": 0.002,
   399→        "latency_ms": 450,
   400→    },
   401→    skill_name="research",
   402→    agent_id="researcher-1"
   403→)
   404→```
   405→
   406→### Querying Metrics (Dashboards + Analysis)
   407→```python
   408→# Real-time dashboard (5-min rolling window)
   409→snapshot = agg.get_metrics_snapshot()
   410→print(f"Throughput: {snapshot.avg_tokens_per_sec} tokens/sec")
   411→print(f"Cache hit: {snapshot.cache_hit_rate:.1%}")
   412→print(f"Cost trending: ${snapshot.daily_cost_estimate:.2f}")
   413→
   414→# Historical trends (skill refinement)
   415→skill_metrics = agg.get_skill_metrics("research", days=7)
   416→if skill_metrics.coherence_trend < -0.05:  # Degrading
   417→    logger.warning("research skill coherence degrading, trigger refinement")
   418→```
   419→
   420→### Cost Tracking (Budget Enforcement)
   421→```python
   422→from cohezion.cost_optimization.budget_enforcer import BudgetEnforcer
   423→
   424→enforcer = BudgetEnforcer(monthly_budget_usd=100)
   425→
   426→# Check before execution
   427→can_proceed, remaining = enforcer.check_budget(estimated_tokens=5000)
   428→if not can_proceed:
   429→    logger.info(f"Budget exhausted, {remaining} tokens remain for month")
   430→    action = "defer_or_escalate"
   431→```
   432→
   433→## Data Storage Architecture for Simulations (Session 55 Patterns)
   434→
   435→**Problem**: Universe simulation systems generate large artifacts (model checkpoints, training logs, metrics). Without governance, data accumulates exponentially: 13 GB/session → 13 TB after 10 sessions without controls.
   436→
   437→**Solution**: Three-tier storage strategy with pre-commit enforcement and JourneyTracker registry.
   438→
   439→### Three-Tier Storage Tiers
   440→
   441→**Tier 1: Git (Reproducible Configs)**
   442→- Store: checksums, model configs, training hyperparameters, seed values
   443→- Size: <1 MB per checkpoint (metadata only, not weights)
   444→- Purpose: version control, audit trail, reproducibility
   445→- Retention: permanent (part of codebase history)
   446→- Example: `data/flume/session55_config.json` (metadata, no weights)
   447→
   448→**Tier 2: SurrealDB (Queryable Index)**
   449→- Store: artifact metadata (path, size, checksum, lifetime, retention_policy)
   450→- Purpose: fast queries ("find all checkpoints from Session 55"), lifecycle management
   451→- Retention: rolling window (100K records = ~10 sessions at typical scale)
   452→- Query latency: <5 ms
   453→- Example: `JourneyTracker.query(session_id="session-55", tier="external")`
   454→
   455→**Tier 3: External (Large Artifacts)**
   456→- Store: checkpoint weights, large run artifacts (>50 MB)
   457→- Backends: s3, gdrive, local NVMe archive
   458→- Purpose: scalable storage, cost-managed archival
   459→- Retention: policy-driven (30-90 days for research, longer for production)
   460→- Example: `s3://cohezion-data/session-55/checkpoint-ep50.pt`
   461→
   462→### Enforcement: Pre-Commit Hook
   463→
   464→```bash
   465→# .git/hooks/pre-commit
   466→# Block commits if >50 MB files detected without external artifact registration
   467→
   468→git diff --cached --name-only | while read file; do
   469→  size=$(git cat-file -s ":0:$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%z "$file" 2>/dev/null)
   470→  if [ "$size" -gt 52428800 ]; then  # 50 MB
   471→    echo "ERROR: Large artifact requires external storage registration"
   472→    echo "Fix: uv run cohezion artifact register --path '$file' --tier external"
   473→    exit 1
   474→  fi
   475→done
   476→```
   477→
   478→Cost: ~100 ms per commit | Benefit: prevents exponential accumulation
   479→
   480→### JourneyTracker Artifact Registration
   481→
   482→```python
   483→from cohezion.compound.journey_tracker import JourneyTracker
   484→
   485→JourneyTracker.record_artifact(
   486→  session_id="session-55",
   487→  artifact_type="checkpoint",
   488→  path="data/flume/session55_run3.pt",
   489→  size_bytes=234_567_890,
   490→  tier="external",  # git|surreal|external
   491→  checksum="sha256:abcd1234",
   492→  lifetime_days=30,
   493→  retention_policy="research",
   494→  tags=["flume", "vae", "training"]
   495→)
   496→
   497→# Query for lifecycle management
   498→artifacts = JourneyTracker.query(tier="external", older_than_days=7)
   499→for artifact in artifacts:
   500→  if artifact.is_expired():
   501→    notify_ops(f"Archive cleanup due: {artifact.path}")
   502→```
   503→
   504→### Recovery Procedure (Deterministic Replay)
   505→
   506→```python
   507→# Recover any historical state in <5 minutes
   508→checkpoint = CheckpointRepo.get_by_seed(seed=42, session="session-55")
   509→state = torch.load(checkpoint.git_ref)
   510→vae = FlumVAETrainer.from_checkpoint(state, continue_training=True)
   511→# Deterministically reproducible from this point
   512→```
   513→
   514→### Success Metrics
   515→
   516→| Metric | Target | Mechanism |
   517→|--------|--------|-----------|
   518→| Committed files/session | <50 MB | Pre-commit hook enforces tier assignment |
   519→| Artifact discoverability | <5 ms | SurrealDB queries on session_id, timestamp |
   520→| Recovery time | <5 min | Deterministic seed + checkpoint lineage |
   521→| Audit trail completeness | 100% | JourneyTracker registers every artifact |
   522→| Storage cost | <$5/10 sessions | Free Git + SurrealDB, s3 for >90-day archive |
   523→
   524→### Implementation Notes
   525→
   526→- **Backward compatible**: JourneyTracker logging is optional (try/except wrapper)
   527→- **Graceful degradation**: If SurrealDB unavailable, falls back to JSONL queries
   528→- **Non-blocking**: All tracking operations non-blocking (won't crash system if unavailable)
   529→- **Reusable patterns**: See `/vaults/cohezion-vault/patterns/` for extracted patterns
   530→
   531→### Related PRIME Skills
   532→
   533→- `UNIVERSE_SIMULATION_PERSISTENCE_PRIME.md`: Complete specification with ROI analysis
   534→- See: `REDACTED.md`
   535→
   536→---
   537→
   538→## Common Debugging Scenarios
   539→
   540→### Scenario: Tests Pass Individually but Fail in Suite
   541→**Root cause**: Singleton pollution in conftest.py fixtures
   542→```bash
   543→# Fix: Verify singleton reset is running
   544→grep -n "_vae_trainer\|_rl_policy\|handlers.clear" tests/conftest.py
   545→
   546→# Debug: Run single test module to verify
   547→uv run pytest tests/compound/test_executor.py -v
   548→# If passes → singleton issue
   549→# If fails → logic bug
   550→```
   551→
   552→### Scenario: Flaky Test with Random Seed Issues
   553→**Root cause**: FLUME VAE or numpy random state not reset
   554→```python
   555→# In your test:
   556→import numpy as np
   557→from cohezion.api import reset_flume_vae
   558→
   559→@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
   560→def reset_random():
   561→    np.random.seed(42)
   562→    reset_flume_vae()
   563→    yield
   564→```
   565→
   566→### Scenario: Ollama Timeout in Tests
   567→**Root cause**: Test is hitting live Ollama instead of mock
   568→```python
   569→# Fix: Mock at source
   570→@patch("cohezion.swarm.compound_client.get_compound_client")
   571→def test_my_thing(mock_client):
   572→    mock_client.return_value = AsyncMock()  # Never talks to real Ollama
   573→```
   574→
   575→### Scenario: Journey Tracking Missing from Logs
   576→**Root cause**: Non-blocking try/except swallowed the error
   577→```python
   578→# Debug: Temporarily make it blocking
   579→try:
   580→    tracker.record_transition(...)
   581→except Exception as e:
   582→    logger.error(f"Journey tracking: {e}")  # See the actual error
   583→    raise  # Temporarily, to find issue
   584→```
   585→
   586→### Scenario: Token Count Doesn't Match Estimate
   587→**Root cause**: Cost tracker using wrong model rate
   588→```python
   589→# Verify cost is being computed
   590→agg = GlobalMetricsAggregator()
   591→metrics = agg.get_metrics_snapshot()
   592→
   593→# Check: Are costs accumulating?
   594→if metrics.total_cost_usd == 0.0:
   595→    logger.warning("Cost tracking not working, check model rates in cost_aware_router.py")
   596→```
   597→
   598→## Quick Lookup
   599→
   600→| Need | Command | File |
   601→|------|---------|------|
   602→| Run tests (all) | `uv run pytest tests/ -q` | `pytest.ini` |
   603→| Run tests (module) | `uv run pytest tests/compound/ -v` | `tests/conftest.py` |
   604→| Format + lint | `make format && make lint` | `Makefile` |
   605→| Check types | `make type-check` | `pyproject.toml` |
   606→| Start API | `uv run uvicorn cohezion.api:app --reload` | `src/cohezion/api/__init__.py` |
   607→| Find skill | `grep -r "skill_name" src/cohezion/skills/` | `skill_registry.json` |
   608→| Debug journeys | `JourneyTracker.get_journey(agent_id)` | `src/cohezion/compound/journey_tracker.py` |
   609→| Check alignment | `RequestAlignmentAnalyzer.analyze(...)` | `src/cohezion/compound/request_alignment_analyzer.py` |

     9→# Git Workflow Rules
    10→
    11→- Never commit directly to `main` — always use feature branches
    12→- Branch naming: `feature/*`, `fix/*`, `refactor/*`, `docs/*`, `improve/*`, `session-*`
    13→- Conventional commit messages required: `feat:`, `fix:`, `refactor:`, `test:`, `docs:`, `chore:`, `perf:`, `ci:`
    14→- No emoji in commit messages
    15→- Subject line under 72 characters, imperative mood

    18→- Never force-push to `main`
    19→- Remote: GitHub (git@github.com:manderson240/cohezion.git)
    20→- Branching model: **GitHub Flow** — `main` is the single long-lived branch; all work happens on feature branches merged via pull requests
    21→- Use git worktrees for Claude sessions: `git worktree add ~/dev/cohezion-session-{N} -b session-{N}-{phase}`
    22→- Reconsider GitHub Flow when: shipping to users who need stable versions → add GitFlow tagging; multiple contributors → add branch protection + CODEOWNERS; need hotfixes without new features → create vX.Y.Z hotfix branches

## Step 1.4: Design Decisions

Based on my exploration, I've found:

**Current State:**
- CLAUDE.md is 610 lines with no @ file references
- Files like `tests/conftest.py`, `.agent/CONSTITUTION.md`, `pyproject.toml` are mentioned as text but not @ linked
- .claude/rules/ already has specialized rule files with path-based triggers
- No CLAUDE.local.md exists yet
- Common imports point to key modules: agents.base, swarm_types, journey_tracker, surreal_client

**Key files that should get @ references (based on frequency and criticality):**
- **Governance:** `.agent/CONSTITUTION.md`, `.agent/COHEZION_CHARTER.md`, `HARDWARE_PROFILE_PRIME.md`
- **Testing:** `tests/conftest.py`, `pytest.ini`
- **Config:** `pyproject.toml`, `.mcp.json`
- **Architecture:** Core modules from src/cohezion/

   248→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |

   251→| CLAUDE.local.md accidentally committed | Low | Low | Add to .gitignore; document clearly that it's personal only |
   252→| Context bloat from too many @ references | Low | Medium | Only @ reference files that are frequently needed; CODE_MAP.md exists for deep dives |
   253→| Inline @ references duplicate section | Low | Low | Keep @ Reference Files section as canonical; inline mentions are contextual supplements |

   100→### ⚡ Architecture at a Glance
   101→| Layer | Components | Entry |
   102→|-------|-----------|-------|
   103→| **Compound** | Executor, SkillRefiner, RetrospectionEngine, JourneyTracker | `CompoundExecutor` |
   104→| **Swarm** | TeamOrchestrator, ExecutionOrchestrator, DynamicModelRouter | `TeamExecutor` |
   105→| **Cache** | SemanticCache (L1 hash + L2 cosine + L3 vault, 95%+ hit rate) | `SemanticCache` |
   106→| **Cost Opt** | CostAwareRouter (27.3% savings), BudgetEnforcer, ModelQualityClassifier | `CostAwareRouter` |
   107→| **Persistence** | SessionPersistence (vault + JSONL), MetricsCollector, DegradationDetector | `SessionManager` |
   108→| **Knowledge** | Vault-First (decisions/patterns/experiments), auto-compiled MEMORY.md | `vault_find_relevant_context` |
   109→
   110→### ⚡ Quick Reference
   111→- **Language**: Python 3.13+ | **Package Manager**: `uv` (never bare python)
   112→- **DB**: SurrealDB (ws://localhost:8000) | **API**: FastAPI :8080
   113→- **Tests**: 3,147 collected, 3,132 passing, 0 collection errors | **Coverage**: html report in `htmlcov/`
   114→- **CI**: GitHub Actions (`.github/workflows/`) | `make lint-check && uv run pytest` before commit
   115→- **Entry point**: `cohezion = "cohezion.__main__:main"`

11-step compound execution pipeline (RequestAlignmentAnalyzer → GlobalMetricsAggregator → DegradationDetector → JourneyTracker)\n- @src/cohezion/compound/journey_tracker.py - 12D universe position tracking, state transitions, rollback checkpoints\n- @src/cohezion/compound/skill_refiner.py - PRIME skill definition updates based on RetrospectionEngine learnings\n- @src/cohezion/compound/request_alignment_analyzer.py - Coherence checking before execution (HIHO threshold: 0.5)\n- @src/cohezion/compound/retrospection_engine.py - Extract learnings from executions, flag anomalies for skill refinement\n- @src/cohezion/compound/global_metrics_aggregator.py - Track efficiency, cost, quality across all agents and executions\n\n---\n\n## Swarm Orchestration Layer\n\n**Multi-agent coordination, cost routing, model selection.**\n\n- @src/cohezion/swarm/team_executor.py - Multi-agent team orchestration with ExecutionOrchestrator integration\n- @src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py - Dynamic model routing for 27.3% cost savings\n- @src/cohezion/swarm/dynamic_model_router.py - Quality-aware model selection based on task requirements\n- @src/cohezion/swarm/execution_orchestrator.py - Coordinates execution across team members with alignment checks\n\n---\n\n## Caching Layer\n\n**L1/L2/L3 semantic cache with 95%+ hit rate.**\n\n- @src/cohezion/cache/semantic_cache.py - Three-tier caching: L1 hash + L2 cosine similarity + L3 vault lookup\n- @src/cohezion/cache/cache_manager.py - Cache lifecycle management, eviction policies\n\n---\n\n## Cost Optimization Layer\n\n**Budget enforcement, model quality classification.**\n\n- @src/cohezion/cost_optimization/budget_enforcer.py - Monthly budget caps, cost tracking per execution\n- @src/cohezion/cost_optimization/model_quality_classifier.py - Classify tasks by quality requirements to select appropriate models\n\n---\n\n## Persistence Layer\n\n**SurrealDB integration, checkpoints, session recovery.**\n\n- @src/cohezion/persistence/surreal_client.py - SurrealDB client for graph storage and queries\n- @src/cohezion/persistence/session_manager.py - Session persistence with vault + JSONL fallback\n- @src/cohezion/persistence/checkpoint_manager.py - Deterministic checkpoint storage and recovery\n\n---\n\n## API Layer\n\n**FastAPI backend with FastMCP patterns.**\n\n- @src/cohezion/api/__init__.py - FastAPI app initialization, MCP server setup (40+ tools proven pattern)\n- @src/cohezion/api/routes_compound.py - Compound engineering endpoints (/compound, /skills, /retrospective)\n- @src/cohezion/api/routes_swarm.py - Swarm orchestration endpoints (team execution, routing)\n- @src/cohezion/api/routes_journeys.py - Journey tracking and 12D universe navigation\n\n---\n\n## FLUME VAE Layer\n\n**256D latent space for experience encoding.**\n\n- @src/cohezion/flume/flume_vae.py - Variational autoencoder for trajectory compression\n- @src/cohezion/flume/experience_pipeline.py - Experience collection and encoding pipeline\n- @src/cohezion/flume/trajectory_stats.py - Trajectory analysis and statistics\n\n---\n\n## Knowledge Management\n\n**Vault-first knowledge persistence and retrieval.**\n\n- @src/cohezion/knowledge_graph/vault_integration.py - {
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/CLAUDE.local.md",
  "content": "# CLAUDE.local.md - Personal Customization\n\n**⚠️ THIS FILE IS FOR YOUR PERSONAL USE ONLY - DO NOT COMMIT TO GIT**\n\nThis file allows you to customize Claude's behavior for your specific Cohezion development workflows. Add personal preferences, custom shortcuts, and context loading strategies here.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Personal Development Workflows\n\n### Custom Test Fixtures for Your Work\n\nWhen working on a specific subsystem, load your most-used fixtures automatically:\n\n```markdown\n# When I say \"test compound\", automatically load:\n- @tests/compound/test_executor.py - My main test suite\n- @tests/conftest.py - Shared fixtures I always need\n- @src/cohezion/compound/executor.py - The implementation I'm testing\n\n# Personal test data setup:\nMy test data lives in `tests/fixtures/my_data.json`. Use this when I ask you to \"test with my data\".\n```\n\n### Debug Configurations\n\n**When debugging flaky tests:**\n```markdown\nALWAYS load @tests/conftest.py first to check singleton resets.\nCheck these three in order:\n1. FLUME VAE reset: `cohezion.api._vae_trainer = None`\n2. RL Policy reset: `cohezion.api._rl_policy = None`\n3. Logger handlers: `logging.getLogger().handlers.clear()`\n```\n\n**When debugging cost tracking:**\n```markdown\nLoad these files together:\n- @src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py - Router logic\n- @src/cohezion/cost_optimization/budget_enforcer.py - Budget caps\n- @tests/swarm/test_cost_aware_router.py - Tests to verify behavior\n\nAlways check: Are model rates up-to-date? (See cost_aware_router.py line 45)\n```\n\n### My Common Commands\n\n**Personal shortcuts I use:**\n```bash\n# Start dev environment (my preferred ports)\nuv run uvicorn cohezion.api:app --reload --port 8081  # I use 8081, not 8080\n\n# Run only my team's tests\nuv run pytest tests/compound/ tests/swarm/ -v  # Skip integration tests unless asked\n\n# Quick type check for files I'm working on\nbasedpyright src/cohezion/compound/ src/cohezion/swarm/  # Faster than full project\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Context Optimization Tips\n\n### Strategic @ Mention Loading\n\n**⚡ Token-Efficient Pattern:** Load files progressively, not all at once.\n\n**Instead of this (wastes context):**\n```\n\"Explain the entire compound engineering system\"\n[Loads 20+ files, most irrelevant]\n```\n\n**Do this (focused, efficient):**\n```\n\"Explain the compound executor's 11-step pipeline\"\n→ Load ONLY @src/cohezion/compound/executor.py\n\n\"How does it track journeys?\"\n→ NOW load @src/cohezion/compound/journey_tracker.py\n```\n\n### When Working on a Feature\n\n**Progressive context loading strategy:**\n\n1. **Start narrow:** @ mention ONLY the file you're modifying\n2. **Expand as needed:** Add dependencies when you hit unclear integrations\n3. **Clean up:** Drop unused @ mentions after they're no longer relevant\n\n**Example - Adding a new skill:**\n```markdown\nStep 1: \"I want to add a skill for X. Check @src/cohezion/skills/skill_registry.json\"\nStep 2: \"Show me a similar skill\" → Load ONE existing skill file as template\nStep 3: Implement (don't load anything else yet)\nStep 4: \"Test it\" → NOW load @tests/conftest.py for fixtures\n```\n\n### Handling Large Files (>500 lines)\n\n**Problem:** Large files consume massive context. **Solution:** Ask for specific functions/classes.\n\n```markdown\n# ❌ WASTEFUL (loads 800-line file):\n\"Explain @src/cohezion/api/__init__.py\"\n\n# ✅ EFFICIENT (gets only what you need):\n\"Show me the FastAPI app initialization in @src/cohezion/api/__init__.py\"\n\"What MCP tools are registered in @src/cohezion/api/__init__.py?\"\n```\n\n### Edge Case: Context Near Limit\n\n**When approaching 90% context:**\n\n1. **Summarize and hand off** - Ask Claude to write a continuation file\n2. **Don't rush** - Quality > speed. A clean handoff beats rushed completion.\n3. **Trust the system** - Context limits are checkpoints, not failures\n\n**What to include in handoff:**\n```markdown\n- Exact line numbers where I left off\n- Specific command I was running when context filled\n- What error I was debugging (if applicable)\n- Next 1-2 concrete steps\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Integration with Tools\n\n### IDE Integration (VS Code / Cursor)\n\n**Use @ mentions in comments for IDE navigation:**\n```python\n# See @src/cohezion/compound/executor.py:45 for implementation details\n# TODO: Refactor this to match pattern in @tests/compound/test_executor.py:120\n```\n\n**Custom VS Code tasks (add to .vscode/tasks.json - NOT committed):**\n```json\n{\n  \"label\": \"Ask Claude to review this file\",\n  \"type\": \"shell\",\n  \"command\": \"echo '@${relativeFile} - Review for bugs and improvements' | pbcopy\",\n  \"presentation\": {\"reveal\": \"never\"}\n}\n```\n\n### Git Hooks Integration\n\n**Pre-commit workflow with Claude:**\n```bash\n# In your personal git hooks (not the project's):\n# .git/hooks/pre-commit\n\n# Ask Claude to review staged changes before committing\ngit diff --staged > /tmp/staged.diff\n# Then in Claude: \"Review these changes for issues: [paste diff]\"\n# Load relevant @ files if Claude needs more context\n```\n\n### Custom Scripts\n\n**My personal Cohezion helper scripts (in ~/bin/, not project):**\n\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\n# ~/bin/cohezion-test-focused\n# Run tests only for files I've modified today\n\nmodified_files=$(git diff --name-only --diff-filter=d origin/main...HEAD | grep \"^src/cohezion\")\nfor file in $modified_files; do\n  test_file=\"tests/${file#src/cohezion/}\"\n  test_file=\"${test_file%.py}_test.py\"\n  if [ -f \"$test_file\" ]; then\n    echo \"Testing $test_file\"\n    uv run pytest \"$test_file\" -v\n  fi\ndone\n```\n\n**Usage with Claude:**\n```markdown\n\"I ran `cohezion-test-focused` and got these failures: [paste output]\"\n→ Claude loads only the relevant test files to debug\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Compound Engineering Workflows\n\n### Skill Refinement Context\n\n**When refining a PRIME skill:**\n```markdown\nALWAYS load these together:\n- @src/cohezion/skills/<skill-name>.md - The skill definition\n- @src/cohezion/compound/skill_refiner.py - Refinement logic\n- @src/cohezion/compound/retrospection_engine.py - How learnings are extracted\n\nAsk: \"What learnings from recent executions should update this skill?\"\n```\n\n### Journey Tracking Debugging\n\n**When journey tracking data looks wrong:**\n```markdown\nLoad in this order:\n1. @src/cohezion/compound/journey_tracker.py - Core tracking logic\n2. @src/cohezion/core/persistence/surreal_client.py - DB persistence\n3. @tests/compound/test_journey_tracker.py - Expected behavior tests\n\nCheck: Is SurrealDB running? `curl http://localhost:8000/health`\n```\n\n### Alignment Issues (Coherence <0.5)\n\n**When request alignment analyzer flags low coherence:**\n```markdown\nDebug checklist:\n1. Load @src/cohezion/compound/request_alignment_analyzer.py\n2. Check: What was the coherence score? (in logs or error message)\n3. Load the skill definition being evaluated\n4. Ask: \"Why is this skill's coherence below HIHO threshold?\"\n\nCommon causes:\n- Skill definition too broad (needs decomposition)\n- Missing required parameters in request\n- Agent's historical performance degraded (check thermal metrics)\n```\n\n### Cost Tracking Verification\n\n**My personal cost monitoring workflow:**\n```markdown\nEvery Friday, check cost metrics:\n\n1. \"Show cost breakdown for this week\"\n   → Load @src/cohezion/cost_optimization/budget_enforcer.py\n   → Run: `python -c \"from cohezion.compound.global_metrics_aggregator import GlobalMetricsAggregator; print(GlobalMetricsAggregator().get_metrics_snapshot())\"`\n\n2. If costs seem high:\n   → Load @src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py\n   → Ask: \"Which models were used most? Are we routing efficiently?\"\n\n3. Target: Stay under $X/month (my personal budget)\n```\n\n### Multi-Agent Swarm Debugging\n\n**When swarm execution behaves unexpectedly:**\n```markdown\nProgressive debugging:\n1. Load @src/cohezion/swarm/team_orchestrator.py - Orchestration logic\n2. Check logs for: Which agents were selected? What was the routing decision?\n3. If routing wrong: Load @src/cohezion/swarm/dynamic_model_router.py\n4. If coordination wrong: Load @src/cohezion/swarm/team_orchestrator.py\n5. If cost wrong: Load @src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py\n\nEdge case: If swarm selects same agent repeatedly (no diversity):\n→ Check thermal metrics in journey tracker (agent might be degraded)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Personal Notes & Preferences\n\n### My Coding Style Preferences\n\n```markdown\n- I prefer explicit over implicit (always add type hints, even when obvious)\n- When suggesting refactoring, show BEFORE/AFTER diffs, not just \"do this\"\n- For test failures, always show the EXACT assertion that failed with line numbers\n```\n\n### My Work Context\n\n```markdown\n**Current focus areas:**\n- [Update this as your work changes]\n- Example: \"Working on improving semantic cache hit rates (Feb 2026)\"\n\n**Files I touch most often:**\n- @src/cohezion/cache/semantic_cache.py\n- @tests/cache/test_semantic_cache.py\n- [Add your frequently-modified files]\n\n**My typical questions:**\n- \"Why did cache miss?\" → Auto-load semantic_cache.py\n- \"Test failed in suite but not alone\" → Auto-load conftest.py\n```\n\n### Environment-Specific Settings\n\n```markdown\n**My local setup:**\n- SurrealDB at custom port: ws://localhost:8001 (not 8000)\n- Ollama models I have installed: deepseek-r1:7b, qwen3-coder:14b (not 30b/70b)\n- Python: 3.13.1 via uv\n\n**Adjust suggestions accordingly:**\n- Don't suggest deepseek-r1:70b (I don't have it)\n- My SurrealDB connection needs port override\n```\n\n---\n\n## Quick Commands I Use\n\n```markdown\n# Reload this file's customizations\n\"Use settings from @CLAUDE.local.md\"\n\n# Load my common context\n\"Load my cache work context\" → semantic_cache.py + tests + conftest.py\n\n# Check my typical suspects when tests fail\n\"Check singleton pollution\" → @tests/conftest.py\n\n# My preferred test command\n\"Run my tests\" → uv run pytest tests/cache/ tests/compound/ -q\n```\n\n---\n\n## Tips for Editing This File\n\n1. **Keep it updated** - Remove stale sections when your focus shifts\n2. **Use @ mentions liberally** - They make loading context instant\n3. **Document your patterns** - If you ask the same question weekly, add it here\n4. **Stay organized** - Group related customizations together\n5. **Version it privately** - Consider keeping this in a private dotfiles repo (NOT the project repo)\n\n**Remember:** This file is YOUR personal assistant configuration. Customize it to match YOUR workflow, not a generic template.\n"
}

  "new_string": "---\n\n## Context Management Best Practices\n\n**Effective context management with Claude Code improves development speed and reduces token usage.**\n\n### Three CLAUDE.md File Locations\n\nClaude Code recognizes three different CLAUDE.md files, each serving a specific purpose:\n\n| Location | Scope | Purpose | Version Control |\n|----------|-------|---------|-----------------|\n| `CLAUDE.md` | **Project-wide** (shared) | Official project guidance, architecture, coding standards | ✅ Committed to git |\n| `CLAUDE.local.md` | **Personal** (this repo only) | Your customizations, shortcuts, workflows for this project | ❌ Gitignored (personal) |\n| `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` | **Global** (all projects) | Personal settings applied to every project on your machine | ❌ Not in project |\n\n**When to use which:**\n- **CLAUDE.md**: Add patterns/rules that benefit all team members\n- **CLAUDE.local.md**: Your personal dev shortcuts, custom test data, debug configs\n- **~/.claude/CLAUDE.md**: Personal preferences that apply across ALL your projects\n\n**Note:** CLAUDE.local.md is in .gitignore and should NEVER be committed. It's for personal use only.\n\n### @ Reference Syntax and Best Practices\n\n**@ mentions automatically load file contents into context.** Use them strategically to avoid context bloat.\n\n**Basic syntax:**\n```markdown\n@path/to/file.py - Load this file's contents\n@docs/CODE_MAP.md - Load CODE_MAP for source navigation\n```\n\n**Best practices:**\n\n1. **Load progressively, not all at once:**\n   ```markdown\n   ❌ Bad: \"Explain the entire compound engineering system\"\n   ✅ Good: \"Explain @src/cohezion/compound/executor.py's 11-step pipeline\"\n   ```\n\n2. **Use CODE_MAP for source discovery:**\n   - First, check @docs/CODE_MAP.md to find relevant files\n   - Then @ mention only the specific files you need\n\n3. **For large files (>500 lines), request specific sections:**\n   ```markdown\n   \"Show the FastAPI app initialization in @src/cohezion/api/__init__.py\"\n   (NOT: \"Explain @src/cohezion/api/__init__.py\")\n   ```\n\n4. **Drop unused @ mentions:**\n   - After you've learned what you needed from a file, stop mentioning it\n   - Only keep @ mentions for files actively being modified\n\n### Using CODE_MAP.md for Source Navigation\n\n**@docs/CODE_MAP.md provides @ references organized by architecture layer.**\n\n**How to use it:**\n\n1. **Discover which files to load:**\n   ```markdown\n   \"I'm working on caching. What files should I look at?\"\n   → Check @docs/CODE_MAP.md under \"Caching Layer\"\n   ```\n\n2. **Load specific subsystem:**\n   ```markdown\n   \"Load the compound engineering layer\"\n   → Mentions files from CODE_MAP's Compound section\n   ```\n\n3. **Navigate dependencies:**\n   ```markdown\n   \"How does @src/cohezion/compound/journey_tracker.py integrate with\n    @src/cohezion/core/persistence/surreal_client.py?\"\n   ```\n\n### CLAUDE.local.md Examples\n\n**Create CLAUDE.local.md in the project root for personal customizations.**\n\nExample contents (customize to your workflow):\n\n```markdown\n# My Personal Cohezion Settings\n\n## Quick Commands\n- \"Run my tests\" → uv run pytest tests/cache/ tests/compound/ -q\n- \"Load cache context\" → @src/cohezion/cache/semantic_cache.py + tests\n\n## Debug Shortcuts\nWhen debugging flaky tests, ALWAYS load @tests/conftest.py first\n\n## My Environment\n- SurrealDB at custom port: ws://localhost:8001\n- Ollama models installed: deepseek-r1:7b, qwen3-coder:14b\n```\n\n**See CLAUDE.local.md template for more examples** (it's gitignored, so you can modify freely).\n\n### Common Patterns\n\n**Pattern 1: Feature development with minimal context**\n```markdown\n1. Start: \"I want to add feature X. Check @src/cohezion/skills/skill_registry.json\"\n2. Template: \"Show me a similar skill\" → Load ONE example\n3. Implement: Don't load anything else yet\n4. Test: NOW load @tests/conftest.py for fixtures\n```\n\n**Pattern 2: Debugging test failures**\n```markdown\n1. \"Why did this test fail?\" → Load ONLY the failing test file\n2. If unclear: Load @tests/conftest.py (check singleton resets)\n3. If still unclear: Load the implementation file being tested\n```\n\n**Pattern 3: Cost optimization work**\n```markdown\n1. @docs/CODE_MAP.md (find cost-related files)\n2. @src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py (router logic)\n3. @src/cohezion/cost_optimization/budget_enforcer.py (if needed)\n```\n\n### Handling Context Limits\n\n**At 90% context usage:**\n1. Finish current task with quality (don't rush)\n2. Ask Claude to write a continuation file with exact state\n3. Trigger session handoff\n4. **Trust the system** - context limits are checkpoints, not failures\n\n**What to include in handoff:**\n- Exact file:line where you left off\n- Specific error or command being debugged\n- Next 1-2 concrete steps\n- Don't compress for token savings - clarity > brevity\n\n### Tips for New Contributors\n\n1. **Read CLAUDE.md first** - Understand project architecture and conventions\n2. **Create CLAUDE.local.md** - Add your personal shortcuts and preferences\n3. **Use @ mentions sparingly** - Load only what you need, when you need it\n4. **Check CODE_MAP.md** - Find source files by subsystem, not by guessing paths\n5. **Update CLAUDE.md** - If you discover patterns others would benefit from, add them to CLAUDE.md (shared)\n\n---\n\n**Last Updated**: 2026-02-10"
}

   115→Vendor `0x1002` = AMD. Prefer GTT over VRAM path. If GTT within 5% of system RAM → UMA. Scoring: `vram_score = min(system_ram_gb / 64.0, 2.0)`.

   178→
   179→## Phase 3: Ollama-Powered Specialist Pipeline (2026-02-06)
   180→
   181→### Learning 97: Weight Bridge Layer Collapse
   182→
   183→PolicyNetwork has 3 linear layers but Rust FlumePhysics accepts only 2. Collapse intermediate layer via matrix multiplication: `w2 = mean_head.weight @ shared[2].weight`, `b2 = mean_head.bias + mean_head.weight @ shared[2].bias`. LayerNorm defaults: gamma=ones, beta=0.5 (HIHO target). Note: `shared[2]` is the second Linear in nn.Sequential (index 0=Linear, 1=ReLU, 2=Linear, 3=ReLU).
   184→
   185→### Learning 98: Ruff Hook Import Fights
   186→
   187→PostToolUse ruff hook auto-removes "unused" imports. Conditional usage is not recognized as "used." Fix: use the import in a type annotation (`self._exporter: CheckpointExporter | None`) which ruff always considers "used."
   188→
   189→### Learning 99: Deterministic Navigator for Simulation
   190→
   191→For stable mass sim, use deterministic mean action from PolicyNetwork (no Gaussian sampling), scaled by action_scale. Sampling introduces too much noise for batch simulation with thousands of agents.
   192→
   193→### Learning 100: Democratic Debate for Structured Output
   194→
   195→DemocraticDebate extracts numeric hyperparameters from free-text consensus via regex. Bounds enforcement (clamping + rounding to powers of 2 for hidden_dim) prevents debate hallucination from producing invalid params.
   196→
   197→### Learning 101: Pipeline Graceful Degradation
   198→
   199→9-step bash pipeline with pre-flight checks. If Ollama unavailable, skip analysis/debate/synthesis (4 of 9 steps) but still run training. Scale-specific params via case statement (demo/medium/overnight).
   200→
   201→---
   202→
   203→## Branch Archaeology: The Runaway Files Incident (2026-01-25 — 2026-01-30)
   204→
   205→Mined from `fix/runaway-files-pre-cleanup` and `ops/hygiene` branches before cleanup.
   206→
   207→### Learning 102: The 8.6M Runaway File Catastrophe (2026-01-25)
   208→
   209→Autonomous overnight simulations generated 8.6 million files under `data/overnight/`, `results/`, `renders/`, and `src/cohezion/knowledge_graph/universe_nodes/`. Root cause: agents writing simulation artifacts to tracked directories with no guardrails. IDE indexers froze, git status took minutes, and the git index bloated past 50MB. Prevention: (1) `.gitignore` must cover ALL output directories before any simulation runs, (2) `check-file-count.sh` pre-commit hook blocks commits when untracked files > 1000, (3) never track directories where agents write output.
   210→
   211→### Learning 103: Cascading Cleanup Debt (2026-01-26)
   212→
   213→The first cleanup (7c6d60a) removed 8.6M files but missed ~1000 build artifacts from `apps/dashboard/assets/` (fonts, JS bundles, CodeMirror language files) and a 325MB PDF. Second pass (1c07dcc) caught these by adding `apps/dashboard/assets/` and `**/*.safetensors` to .gitignore. Lesson: cleanup is never one-pass. Each removal reveals the next layer of bloat. Budget 2-3 passes minimum.
   214→
   215→### Learning 104: System Lockup Pattern — GPU Hang (2026-01-27)
   216→
   217→Total system freeze during concurrent web apps + LLM swarm. Root cause chain: (1) VRAM saturation from unthrottled concurrent model loads, (2) `amdgpu` ring reset failure, (3) kernel coredump stall under resource pressure. Required REISUB recovery. Fix: direct sysfs GPU/VRAM monitoring, aggressive PID kill in RED alert, `tune_system.sh` to disable panic-on-oom, prevention of giant coredumps. Key insight: **VRAM is the bottleneck, not RAM. Swarms must be sacrificial; system integrity is primary.**
   218→
   219→### Learning 105: The Untrack & Mine Protocol
   220→
   221→Operational procedure invented during ops/hygiene: (1) identify tracked files that shouldn't be, (2) mine them for knowledge before removal, (3) add to .gitignore, (4) `git rm --cached`, (5) verify git status clean. Critical rule: NEVER delete without reading first. This protocol prevented knowledge loss during the 8.6M file cleanup.
   222→
   223→### Learning 106: .gitignore Layered Defense Pattern
   224→
   225→The .gitignore evolved through 4 iterations across these branches, each adding a new defense layer: (1) output directories (`data/`, `results/`, `renders/`), (2) build artifacts (`assets/`, `*.safetensors`), (3) binary patterns (`*.pt`, `*.pdf`, `*.so`, `*.mp3`), (4) negation rules to protect source (`!src/**/*.py`, `!scripts/**/*.py`). The final pattern: block everything risky at category level, then whitelist specific safe patterns. Order matters — negations must come after the block rule.
   226→
   227→### Learning 107: OMEGA Distiller — Skill Extraction from Success Logs
   228→
   229→Auto-skill-generation concept: parse "MISSION SUCCESS" logs, strip noise/timestamps/paths, extract the "trick" (specific insight that turned failure into success), generalize into PRIME skill format. Template: domain expertise → key concepts → step-by-step instruction → patterns/antipatterns table. Never hardcode variable names from specific missions.
   230→
   231→### Learning 108: Temporal Dilation Under Pressure
   232→
   233→Resource monitor enhancement: `dilation_factor` (1.0 = normal, 0.1 = severe) dynamically slows simulations when system pressure exceeds thresholds. Coordinated with "Desperation Mode" that throttles all non-essential containers at 90% CPU. Swarm operations observe the dilation factor before scheduling new work.
   234→
   235→### Learning 109: pre-commit-hooks Stage Override (2026-02-06)
   236→
   237→The `pre-commit-hooks` repo internally declares `stages: [commit, push]` on all its hooks, overriding `default_stages: [pre-commit]`. Must add explicit `stages: [pre-commit]` to each hook (trailing-whitespace, end-of-file-fixer, check-yaml, check-json) to prevent them from running during push. Without this, pushes take 10+ minutes and modify files mid-push.
   238→
   239→---
   240→
   241→## Phase 5: Live Compound Engineering (2026-02-06)
   242→
   243→### Learning 110: Mock Live Clients at Source Module
   244→
   245→API tests that call `get_compound_client()` hang indefinitely if Ollama is down — the `ResilientOllamaClient` retries with exponential backoff. Fix: `patch("cohezion.swarm.compound_client.get_compound_client", return_value=mock_client)`. Critical: patch at the **source module** (`cohezion.swarm.compound_client`), not the import site (`cohezion.api`), because endpoints use local imports.
   246→
   247→### Learning 111: Compound Module Separation
   248→
   249→The `cohezion.compound` package (`executor`, `feedback_loop`, `metrics`, `persistence`, `config`, `models`, `health`) is distinct from `cohezion.core.compound` (`retrospection`, `skill_refiner`). The former is the live execution runtime; the latter is the analysis/refinement engine. They connect via `RetrospectionEngine.analyze_execution()` accepting duck-typed execution reports.
   250→
   251→### Learning 112: CI Validation as Compound Defense
   252→
   253→4 CI validation scripts (`validate_agents.py`, `validate_skills.py`, `validate_registry.py`, `compound_audit.py`) + GitLab CI pipeline + Makefile targets = layered defense. Each validates a different invariant: agent frontmatter, skill structure, registry consistency, compound loop health. `make ci` runs all in sequence. Pattern: each validation script returns exit code 0/1 for CI compatibility.
   254→
   255→---
   256→
   257→## Phase 6: Autonomic Connectivity Swarm (2026-02-09)
   258→
   259→### Learning 113: Connectivity Scout & Truth Anchors
   260→
   261→Relying on static documentation for service ports (SurrealDB, Vault, Ollama) is a "Fragile Anchor" failure. The 'Connectivity Squad' pattern uses `lsof`/`ss` commands as autonomic diagnostics to establish dynamic "Truth Anchors" in real-time.
   262→
   263→### Learning 114: Heartbeat Service Probing
   264→
   265→Autonomic reliability hinges on the loop: Discover -> Verify -> Monitor. Integrated service heartbeat checks into `ResourceMonitor` using lightweight `curl` probes (`http_code` verification) to detect "Connectivity Drift" without adding library overhead. Dilation logic can now react to service downtime as well as hardware pressure.
   266→
   267→### Learning 115: Decentralized Memory Sovereignty (2026-02-09)
   268→
   269→Unifying Structured Persistence (SurrealDB) with Contextual Retrospectives (Obsidian Vault) creates a decentralized memory layer that transcends the IDE. By using an MCP-backed Vault for machine-readable checkpoints and human-readable mission retrospectives, agents achieve "Interface Sovereignty"—their state is available and identical whether running in a CLI, an API, or a swarm orchestrator. Circular dependency resolution in the `exp_persistence` package was the final barrier to this architectural stability.
   270→
   271→## Learning 119: The God Object Collapse (API Decoupling) (2026-02-10)
   272→
   273→Extracting machine learning and training logic (VAE/RL) from the main `api/__init__.py` entry point into dedicated service modules reduces monolithic sprawl and prevents architectural contagion. Delegating to services allows for independent scaling and safer unit testing of complex logic.
   274→
   275→## Learning 120: Soft Schema Enforcement for Scout Resilience (2026-02-10)
   276→
   277→When dealing with non-deterministic LLM JSON outputs, implementing "Soft Schema" enforcement (using `.get()` with intelligent defaults) prevents catastrophic swarm failures during semantic scouting. This pattern provides a first line of defense before harder Pydantic validation layers.
   278→
   279→## Learning 121: Token-Aware Fiscal Visibility (2026-02-16)
   280→
   281→Implementing `TokenEfficiencyTracker` provides an R-Zero fiscal baseline. Automated cost estimation (Premium ~$10/1M, Economy ~$0.1M, Local $0) enables precise economic trajectory prediction for large-scale agent swarms.
   282→
   283→## Learning 122: Vault-Guided Local Offload (2026-02-16)
   284→
   285→Bridging SurrealDB (high-density thought memory) and Obsidian (high-fidelity learning patterns) via `VaultGuidedRouter` identifies "Stability Anchors." When high context density or existing patterns are detected, local SLMs (e.g., Qwen3-Coder) can safely replace premium models, preserving token credits for high-horizon reasoning.
   286→
   287→## Learning 123: Git Worktree Session Isolation (2026-02-16)
   288→
   289→Mandating worktrees (`WorktreeOrchestrator`) in `/tmp/cohezion_swarm/` is the only way to ensure non-destructive parallel agentic development. Isolated environments prevent repository index corruption and environment drift during long-horizon missions with multiple concurrent agents.
   290→
   291→## Learning 124: Systemd Crash-Loop Prevention (2026-02-18)
   292→
   293→`StartLimitBurst` MUST be in `[Unit]` not `[Service]` (systemd 255+). `EnvironmentFile=` reads literally — no shell expansion. Three-layer defense: (1) `StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec`, (2) resource caps (`MemoryMax`, `CPUQuota`), (3) external guardian timer. The 4-day SurrealDB crash loop (129K+ restarts) was caused by RocksDB MANIFEST corruption with no restart limits.
   294→
   295→## Learning 125: Log Lifecycle Management (2026-02-19)
   296→
   297→Three-layer approach: (1) Extract crash timelines BEFORE purging, (2) Retain via journald + logrotate size limits (`maxsize 100M`, `SystemMaxUse=2G`), (3) Monitor via guardian timer with JSON output. The Feb 10-18 crash loop generated 6.9M events that would have been lost without extraction first.
   298→
   299→## Learning 126: Adversarial Claim Validation (2026-02-19)
   300→
   301→Every completion claim must be independently verified with fresh command execution. Scope must be explicit — "0 errors in new files" vs "entire codebase." Table format (Claim | Actual | Verdict) provides honest, scannable reporting.
   302→
   303→## Learning 127: Phantom Test Failures from Missing Dev Dependencies (2026-02-19)
   304→
   305→Packages installed via `uv pip install` but not in `pyproject.toml[dev]` get dropped by `uv sync`. Missing `pytest-asyncio` caused 453 cascading failures; missing `gymnasium`/`python-jose`/`passlib` caused collection errors. Fix: add to `pyproject.toml` optional-dependencies, use `uv sync --extra dev`. Diagnostic: if tests pass individually but fail in suite, check async test infrastructure before assuming singleton pollution.
   306→
   307→## Learning 128: Import Cascade Failures via Conftest (2026-02-19)
   308→
   309→Top-level imports of heavy optional deps (matplotlib, numpy) in route files cascade through conftest's `import cohezion.api` autouse fixture, breaking ALL tests — not just the importing module's. Fix: lazy imports inside endpoint functions. Rule: never top-level import optional visualization/ML deps in API route files.
   310→
